- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `issues.go`** (and delete the `importIssues` stub)

```go
package qaimport

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
	"mime"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/uyab/lotus/internal/repo"
)

func (im *Importer) importIssues(ctx context.Context, w *repo.QAImportWriter, legacyProject uuid.UUID, st *projectState, rep *Report) error {
	issues, err := im.legacy.Issues(ctx, legacyProject)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	codes := make([]string, len(issues))
	for i, is := range issues {
		codes[i] = is.Code
	}
	keys := AllocateKeys(codes)
	for i, is := range issues {
		title := TruncateRunes(strings.TrimSpace(is.Title), repo.MaxQATitleLength)
		if title == "" {
			title = "Untitled issue"
		}
		var planID, sectionID *uuid.UUID
		if is.ReleaseID != nil {
			if p, ok := st.plans[*is.ReleaseID]; ok {
				planID = &p
			}
		}
		if is.ModuleID != nil {
			if s, ok := st.sections[*is.ModuleID]; ok {
				sectionID = &s
			}
		}
		renumbered := is.Code != "" && keys[i] != keyOf(is.Code)
		description := CleanText(is.Description)
		if renumbered {
			description = WithLegacyCode(description, is.Code)
		}
		id, err := w.InsertIssue(repo.QAIssue{
			ProjectID: st.projectID, Key: keys[i], Title: title, Description: description,
			StepsToReproduce: CleanText(is.Steps), Severity: MapSeverity(is.Severity, is.Priority),
			Status: MapIssueStatus(is.Status), Environment: CleanText(is.Environment), PlanID: planID,
			SectionID: sectionID, AssigneeID: im.user(is.AssigneeID), ReportedBy: im.user(is.CreatedBy),
			Source: "internal", CreatedAt: is.CreatedAt, UpdatedAt: is.UpdatedAt,
		})
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("issue %s %q: %w", is.Code, title, err)
		}
		st.issues[is.ID] = id
		if renumbered {
			rep.warn("issue %s %q renumbered to BUG-%d", is.Code, title, keys[i])
		}
		if err := w.Remember("test_issues", is.ID, id); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		rep.imported("issues", 1)
	}

	links, err := im.legacy.IssueLinks(ctx, legacyProject)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	for _, l := range links {
		itemID, ok1 := st.items[l.DetailID]
		issueID, ok2 := st.issues[l.IssueID]
		if !ok1 || !ok2 {
			rep.skipped("issue_links", "item or issue not imported")
			continue
		}
		if err := w.InsertIssueLink(itemID, issueID, l.CreatedAt); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		rep.imported("issue_links", 1)
	}

	type event struct {
		at     time.Time
		legacy uuid.UUID
		isNote bool
		ev     repo.QAIssueEvent
	}
	var events []event
	comments, err := im.legacy.Comments(ctx, legacyProject)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	for _, c := range comments {
		issueID, ok := st.issues[c.IssueID]
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		events = append(events, event{at: c.CreatedAt, legacy: c.ID, isNote: true, ev: repo.QAIssueEvent{
			IssueID: issueID, Kind: "comment", Body: strings.TrimSpace(c.Body), ActorID: im.user(c.CreatedBy), CreatedAt: c.CreatedAt,
		}})
	}
	logs, err := im.legacy.IssueLogs(ctx, legacyProject)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	for _, l := range logs {
		issueID, ok := st.issues[l.IssueID]
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		from, to := MapIssueStatus(l.From), MapIssueStatus(l.To)
		if from == to {
			rep.skipped("issue_events", "no change")
			continue
		}
		events = append(events, event{at: l.CreatedAt, legacy: l.ID, ev: repo.QAIssueEvent{
			IssueID: issueID, Kind: "status", StatusFrom: &from, StatusTo: &to, ActorID: im.user(l.By), CreatedAt: l.CreatedAt,
		}})
	}
	sort.SliceStable(events, func(i, j int) bool { return events[i].at.Before(events[j].at) })
	for _, e := range events {
		id, err := w.InsertIssueEvent(e.ev)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		table := "test_issue_logs"
		if e.isNote {
			table = "comments"
			st.comments[e.legacy] = id
		}
		if err := w.Remember(table, e.legacy, id); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		rep.imported("issue_events", 1)
	}

	return im.importFiles(ctx, w, legacyProject, st, rep)
}

func (im *Importer) importFiles(ctx context.Context, w *repo.QAImportWriter, legacyProject uuid.UUID, st *projectState, rep *Report) error {
	files, err := im.legacy.Files(ctx, legacyProject)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	root, _ := filepath.Abs(im.opt.FilesDir)
	for _, f := range files {
		if im.opt.FilesDir == "" {
			rep.skipped("attachments", "files not requested")
			continue
		}
		var owner repo.QAAttachmentOwner
		switch f.OwnerModel {
		case `App\Models\Result`:
			if id, ok := st.results[f.OwnerID]; ok {
				owner.ResultID = &id
			}
		case `App\Models\TestIssue`:
			if id, ok := st.issues[f.OwnerID]; ok {
				owner.IssueID = &id
			}
		case `App\Models\Comment`:
			if id, ok := st.comments[f.OwnerID]; ok {
				owner.EventID = &id
			}
		}
		if owner.ResultID == nil && owner.IssueID == nil && owner.EventID == nil {
			rep.skipped("attachments", "owner not imported")
			continue
		}
		src := filepath.Join(root, filepath.FromSlash(strings.TrimPrefix(f.Path, "/")))
		if rel, err := filepath.Rel(root, src); err != nil || strings.HasPrefix(rel, "..") {
			rep.skipped("attachments", "path outside files dir")
			continue
		}
		file, err := os.Open(src)
		if errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
			rep.skipped("attachments", "file missing")
			rep.warn("attachment %s missing at %s", f.ID, f.Path)
			continue
		}
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		key := fmt.Sprintf("qa/%s/legacy/%s", im.opt.OrgID, f.ID)
		var size int64
		if im.opt.DryRun {
			// A dry run writes nothing, the blob store included.
			info, err := file.Stat()
			file.Close()
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
			size = info.Size()
		} else {
			size, err = im.store.Put(ctx, key, file)
			file.Close()
			if err != nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("copy %s: %w", f.Path, err)
			}
		}
		name := f.Name
		if name == "" {
			name = filepath.Base(f.Path)
		}
		ctype := mime.TypeByExtension(strings.ToLower(filepath.Ext(name)))
		if ctype == "" {
			ctype = "application/octet-stream"
		}
		id, err := w.InsertAttachment(repo.QAAttachment{
			ResultID: owner.ResultID, IssueID: owner.IssueID, EventID: owner.EventID,
			FileName: TruncateRunes(name, 255), ContentType: ctype, SizeBytes: size, StorageKey: key, CreatedAt: f.CreatedAt,
		})
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := w.Remember("minio", f.ID, id); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		rep.imported("attachments", 1)
	}
	return nil
}
```

`mime.TypeByExtension(".txt")` returns `text/plain; charset=utf-8` on Linux and macOS; if a CI image lacks `/etc/mime.types`, Go's built-in table still covers `.txt`, `.png`, `.jpg`, `.pdf`.

func TestVerify_CleanAfterImportAndDetectsDrift(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	e.run(t)
	mm, err := qaimport.Verify(ctx, e.db, e.legacy, e.orgID)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(mm) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("mismatches after a clean import: %+v", mm)
	}
	// Drift: delete one imported result.
	withOrg(t, e, func(tx *sqlx.Tx) { tx.Exec(`DELETE FROM qa_results WHERE id = (SELECT id FROM qa_results LIMIT 1)`) })
	mm, _ = qaimport.Verify(ctx, e.db, e.legacy, e.orgID)
	if len(mm) != 1 || mm[0].Field != "results" || mm[0].Legacy != 2 || mm[0].Lotus != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("mismatches = %+v; want one on results", mm)
	}
}

func TestVerify_CatchesAStrayProjectWithQAData(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	e.run(t)
	stray, err := repo.CreateProject(ctx, e.s, repo.NewProject{Slug: "stray", Name: "Stray"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	sec, _ := repo.CreateQASection(ctx, e.s, stray.ID, "S", nil)
	repo.CreateQACase(ctx, e.s, stray.ID, repo.QACaseInput{SectionID: sec.ID, Title: "x"}, nil)
	mm, _ := qaimport.Verify(ctx, e.db, e.legacy, e.orgID)
	if len(mm) != 1 || mm[0].Field != "projects_with_qa_data" || mm[0].Legacy != 1 || mm[0].Lotus != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("mismatches = %+v; want one projects_with_qa_data", mm)
	}
}

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `csvmaps.go`**

```go
package qaimport

import (
	"encoding/csv"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"strings"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

func readCSV(path string, header ...string) ([][]string, error) {
	f, err := os.Open(path)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	defer f.Close()
	rows, err := csv.NewReader(f).ReadAll()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if len(rows) == 0 || len(rows[0]) < len(header) {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s: header must be %s", path, strings.Join(header, ","))
	}
	for i, h := range header {
		if strings.TrimSpace(rows[0][i]) != h {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s: header must be %s", path, strings.Join(header, ","))
		}
	}
	return rows[1:], nil
}

func LoadUsersMap(path string) (map[string]string, error) {
	rows, err := readCSV(path, "legacy_email", "canonical_email")
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := map[string]string{}
	for _, r := range rows {
		out[strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(r[0]))] = strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(r[1]))
	}
	return out, nil
}

func LoadProjectsMap(path string) (map[uuid.UUID]string, error) {
	rows, err := readCSV(path, "legacy_project_id", "lotus_project_slug")
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := map[uuid.UUID]string{}
	for i, r := range rows {
		id, err := uuid.Parse(strings.TrimSpace(r[0]))
		if err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s line %d: %w", path, i+2, err)
		}
		out[id] = strings.TrimSpace(r[1])
	}
	return out, nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `verify.go`**

```go
package qaimport

import (
	"context"
	"sort"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jmoiron/sqlx"

	"github.com/uyab/lotus/internal/repo"
	"github.com/uyab/lotus/internal/repo/legacyqa"
	"github.com/uyab/lotus/internal/tenant"
)

type Mismatch struct {
	LegacyProjectID uuid.UUID
	Project         string
	Field           string
	Legacy, Lotus   int
}

// legacyCounts computes, from the legacy side, what an import of this
// project must have produced — the same inclusion and mapping rules the
// importer applies, so the two sides are comparable number for number.
func legacyCounts(ctx context.Context, r *legacyqa.Reader, pid uuid.UUID) (repo.QACounts, error) {
	out := repo.QACounts{ItemStatus: map[string]int{}, IssueStatus: map[string]int{}}
	cases, err := r.Cases(ctx, pid)
	if err != nil {
		return out, err
	}
	out.Cases = len(cases)
	for _, c := range cases {
		if c.DeletedAt != nil || c.Archived {
			out.ArchivedCases++
		}
	}
	runs, err := r.Runs(ctx, pid)
	if err != nil {
		return out, err
	}
	out.Runs = len(runs)
	details, err := r.Details(ctx, pid)
	if err != nil {
		return out, err
	}
	for _, d := range details {
		out.ItemStatus[MapItemStatus(d.Status)]++
		out.Items++
	}
	results, err := r.Results(ctx, pid)
	if err != nil {
		return out, err
	}
	for _, res := range results {
		if _, ok := MapResultStatus(res.Status); ok {
			out.Results++
		}
	}
	issues, err := r.Issues(ctx, pid)
	if err != nil {
		return out, err
	}
	for _, is := range issues {
		out.IssueStatus[MapIssueStatus(is.Status)]++
		out.Issues++
	}
	comments, err := r.Comments(ctx, pid)
	if err != nil {
		return out, err
	}
	logs, err := r.IssueLogs(ctx, pid)
	if err != nil {
		return out, err
	}
	out.IssueEvents = len(comments)
	for _, l := range logs {
		if MapIssueStatus(l.From) != MapIssueStatus(l.To) {
			out.IssueEvents++
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

func Verify(ctx context.Context, db *sqlx.DB, legacy *legacyqa.Reader, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]Mismatch, error) {
	s := tenant.FromRun(db, orgID)
	done, err := repo.ListQALegacyIDs(ctx, s, "project_done")
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	projects, err := legacy.Projects(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	titles := map[uuid.UUID]string{}
	for _, p := range projects {
		titles[p.ID] = p.Title
	}
	var out []Mismatch
	// Organization-wide: every project holding QA data must be one the
	// import finished. Run verify right after importing, before people start
	// creating QA data in new projects.
	withData, err := repo.CountProjectsWithQAData(ctx, s)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if withData != len(done) {
		out = append(out, Mismatch{Project: "(organization)", Field: "projects_with_qa_data", Legacy: len(done), Lotus: withData})
	}
	for legacyID, projectID := range done {
		want, err := legacyCounts(ctx, legacy, legacyID)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		got, err := repo.QACountsForProject(ctx, s, projectID)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		add := func(field string, l, n int) {
			if l != n {
				out = append(out, Mismatch{LegacyProjectID: legacyID, Project: titles[legacyID], Field: field, Legacy: l, Lotus: n})
			}
		}
		add("cases", want.Cases, got.Cases)
		add("archived_cases", want.ArchivedCases, got.ArchivedCases)
		add("runs", want.Runs, got.Runs)
		add("items", want.Items, got.Items)
		add("results", want.Results, got.Results)
		add("issues", want.Issues, got.Issues)
		add("issue_events", want.IssueEvents, got.IssueEvents)
		for _, st := range []string{"untested", "passed", "failed", "blocked", "skipped"} {
			add("items."+st, want.ItemStatus[st], got.ItemStatus[st])
		}
		for _, st := range repo.QAIssueStatuses {
			add("issues."+st, want.IssueStatus[st], got.IssueStatus[st])
		}
	}
	sort.Slice(out, func(i, j int) bool {
		if out[i].Project != out[j].Project {
			return out[i].Project < out[j].Project
		}
		return out[i].Field < out[j].Field
	})
	return out, nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the command**

Create `cmd/lotus/import_qa.go`:

```go
package main

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"flag"
	"fmt"
	"log"
	"os"
	"time"

	"github.com/uyab/lotus/internal/blob"
	"github.com/uyab/lotus/internal/config"
	"github.com/uyab/lotus/internal/db"
	"github.com/uyab/lotus/internal/qaimport"
	"github.com/uyab/lotus/internal/repo/global"
	"github.com/uyab/lotus/internal/repo/legacyqa"
)

func runImportQA(cfg config.Config, args []string) {
	verify := len(args) > 0 && args[0] == "verify"
	if verify {
		args = args[1:]
	}
	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("import-qa", flag.ExitOnError)
	legacyDSN := fs.String("legacy-dsn", "", "read-only DSN of the legacy QA Tools database (required)")
	orgSlug := fs.String("org", "", "subdomain of the Lotus organization to import into (required)")
	usersMap := fs.String("users-map", "", "CSV legacy_email,canonical_email")
	projectsMap := fs.String("projects-map", "", "CSV legacy_project_id,lotus_project_slug")
	folder := fs.String("folder", "QA Tools (imported)", "folder for newly created projects (empty = root)")
	dormant := fs.String("dormant-before", time.Now().AddDate(-1, 0, 0).Format("2006-01-02"), "no activity since then = no membership")
	closeIdle := fs.String("close-idle-before", time.Now().AddDate(0, 0, -30).Format("2006-01-02"), "close plans idle since before this date (empty = none)")
	filesDir := fs.String("files-dir", "", "mirror of the legacy MinIO bucket (empty = skip attachments)")
	dryRun := fs.Bool("dry-run", false, "run everything, then roll back")
	reportPath := fs.String("report", "", "write the JSON report here")
	fs.Parse(args)
	if *legacyDSN == "" || *orgSlug == "" {
		fs.Usage()
		os.Exit(2)
	}

	ctx := context.Background()
	appDB, err := db.OpenApp(cfg.DatabaseURL)
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer appDB.Close()
	org, err := global.OrgBySubdomain(ctx, appDB, *orgSlug)
	if err != nil || org == nil {
		log.Fatalf("organization %q: %v", *orgSlug, err)
	}
	legacy, err := legacyqa.Open(*legacyDSN)
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer legacy.Close()

	if verify {
		mm, err := qaimport.Verify(ctx, appDB, legacy, org.ID)
		if err != nil {
			log.Fatal(err)
		}
		for _, m := range mm {
			fmt.Printf("%-50s %-20s legacy=%-6d lotus=%d\n", m.Project, m.Field, m.Legacy, m.Lotus)
		}
		if len(mm) > 0 {
			fmt.Printf("%d mismatches\n", len(mm))
			os.Exit(1)
		}
		fmt.Println("verify: ok")
		return
	}

	opt := qaimport.Options{OrgID: org.ID, FolderName: *folder, FilesDir: *filesDir, DryRun: *dryRun}
	if opt.DormantBefore, err = time.Parse("2006-01-02", *dormant); err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("--dormant-before: %v", err)
	}
	if *closeIdle != "" {
		if opt.CloseIdleBefore, err = time.Parse("2006-01-02", *closeIdle); err != nil {
			log.Fatalf("--close-idle-before: %v", err)
		}
	}
	if *usersMap != "" {
		if opt.UsersMap, err = qaimport.LoadUsersMap(*usersMap); err != nil {
			log.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	if *projectsMap != "" {
		if opt.ProjectsMap, err = qaimport.LoadProjectsMap(*projectsMap); err != nil {
			log.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	store, err := blob.NewFS(cfg.BlobDir)
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatal(err)
	}
	rep, runErr := qaimport.New(appDB, legacy, store, opt).Run(ctx)
	out, _ := json.MarshalIndent(rep, "", "  ")
	if *reportPath != "" {
		if err := os.WriteFile(*reportPath, out, 0o644); err != nil {
			log.Printf("write report: %v", err)
		}
	}
	fmt.Println(string(out))
	if runErr != nil {
		log.Fatalf("import stopped: %v (re-run to resume; finished projects are skipped)", runErr)
	}
}
```

In `cmd/lotus/main.go`, replace the `import-legacy` case block's neighbour list by adding:

```go
	case "import-qa":
		runImportQA(cfg, os.Args[2:])
```

and append `|import-qa [verify] --legacy-dsn=<dsn> --org=<subdomain> [...]` to the `usage()` string. Check `global.OrgBySubdomain`'s return shape (it returns `(*Org, error)`); adjust the nil check if it returns `ErrNotFound` instead.

Run: `go test ./internal/qaimport ./cmd/lotus -v && go build ./cmd/lotus && make lint`
Expected: PASS and a built binary.

internal/qaimport          `lotus import-qa`: legacy QA Tools → one organization, plus `verify`

### Task 19: Rehearsal against the production dump and runbook

**Files:**
- Create: `docs/qa-import-runbook.md`, `docs/qa-import/users-map.csv` (the 10 known duplicate people)

This task runs the importer against the real data. It is done by hand; its deliverable is a clean `verify`, a report committed as evidence, and the runbook.

- [ ] **Step 1: Build the users map from the dump**

Run against the restored dump (local Postgres 5416, database `qa_tools_analysis`, see spec §9):

```bash
psql -h localhost -p 5416 -U postgres -d qa_tools_analysis -At -F, -c "
  SELECT lower(u.email), lower(c.email)
  FROM users u JOIN users c ON lower(c.first_name||c.last_name) = lower(u.first_name||u.last_name)
   AND c.id <> u.id AND c.email ILIKE '%@javan.co.id' AND u.email NOT ILIKE '%@javan.co.id'
  ORDER BY 1"
```

Review each line by hand (same name is not proof of same person), then write `docs/qa-import/users-map.csv` with the header `legacy_email,canonical_email`. Expected: about 10 rows (Raditya, Aisha, Latifah, Ervalsa, Aufa, Rahmi, Ishaq, Alvian, Firasyana; Aditomo maps linknet → biz.linknet).

- [ ] **Step 2: Dry run against the dump**

```bash
make db-up && make migrate-up
go run ./cmd/lotus init --org-name=Javan --owner-email=<your email> --slug=javan   # if the dev DB has no org yet
go run ./cmd/lotus import-qa \
  --legacy-dsn='postgres://postgres@localhost:5416/qa_tools_analysis?sslmode=disable' \
  --org=javan --users-map=docs/qa-import/users-map.csv --dry-run --report=/tmp/qa-dry.json
```

Expected (measured on the 2026-09-26 dump during plan validation, without users map or files; about 80 s):

| Report entry | Imported | Skipped |
|---|---|---|
| projects | 169 | 22 without QA data |
| users / memberships | 191 / 72 | memberships: 119 dormant |
| project_members | 4 | |
| sections | 8,867 | 937 deleted and empty |
| cases | 57,896 | |
| plans (plans_closed) | 646 (582) | |
| runs / items / results | 969 / 54,630 / 40,937 | results: 489 not a result |
| issues / issue_events / issue_links | 8,866 / 17,550 / 5,329 | links: 169 item or issue not imported |
| warnings | 289 renumbered cases, 0 renumbered issues, 0 flattened sections | |

Codes such as `TC-LIST-01` and `TC-SEARCH-01` share trailing digits with `TC0001`, which is why about 289 cases are renumbered; each keeps its old code in its notes. With the users map, users drop by about 10 and memberships change accordingly.

If it stops with an error, fix the importer (add a fixture row to `legacy_fixture.sql` reproducing the case, a failing assertion in `importer_test.go`, then the fix), and repeat.

- [ ] **Step 3: Real import into the dev database, then verify**

```bash
go run ./cmd/lotus import-qa --legacy-dsn='postgres://postgres@localhost:5416/qa_tools_analysis?sslmode=disable' \
  --org=javan --users-map=docs/qa-import/users-map.csv --report=docs/qa-import/rehearsal-2026-09.json
go run ./cmd/lotus import-qa verify --legacy-dsn='postgres://postgres@localhost:5416/qa_tools_analysis?sslmode=disable' --org=javan
```

Expected: `verify: ok` (about 7 s). Record the wall-clock time of the import in the runbook (about 80 s on the dump; add the attachment copy time for 2.8 GB) — it sizes the cut-over read-only window.

- [ ] **Step 4: Kill-and-resume check**

Drop and recreate the dev database, start the import in the background, kill it hard after about 20 seconds (`kill -KILL <pid>`; a scripted SIGINT to a background job is ignored), run it again, then `verify`.
Expected: no partially imported project (during validation 31 projects were done when killed, the resume imported the other 138), the second run skips finished projects, totals equal a clean run, and `verify: ok`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the runbook**

Create `docs/qa-import-runbook.md` with these sections, filled with the numbers measured above:

1. **Inputs** — legacy DSN (read-only role, e.g. the existing `ceomonitor` read-only user on `50.50.50.137:5432/qa_tools_dbs`), `users-map.csv`, optional `projects-map.csv` (legacy projects that already exist in Lotus under another slug), MinIO mirror (`mc mirror <legacy-alias>/<bucket> /srv/qa-minio-mirror`).
2. **Rehearsal** — Steps 2–4 of this task against a fresh production dump.
3. **Cut-over** — announce; put QA Tools in read-only mode (maintenance flag, or revoke write grants from its app role); final `mc mirror`; `lotus import-qa` without `--dry-run`; `verify`; spot-check 3 projects in the UI; switch ceo-monitor (Phase 8); announce.
4. **Rollback** — before announcing, delete the imported organization's QA rows (`DELETE FROM qa_… WHERE org_id = …` in dependency order, plus imported projects in the import folder) and re-open QA Tools for writes.
5. **After** — keep the legacy database read-only for 90 days; then drop `qa_legacy_ids` and decommission.

- [ ] **Step 6: Commit**

```bash
git add docs/qa-import-runbook.md docs/qa-import/users-map.csv docs/qa-import/rehearsal-2026-09.json
git commit -m "docs(qa-import): runbook, users map and rehearsal report"
```

## Phases 4–8 — outlines

Each outline below is the **spec for its own detailed plan**. Before starting a phase, run superpowers:writing-plans on it, read the code Phases 1–3 produced, look at the imported data in the UI, and keep every constraint in *Global Constraints*. Every route in these phases:

- sits under `tenant.RequireTenant(), requireSession, loadPrincipal`, then `authz.RequireProjectAccess(projectMemberCheck(d), "id")` for anything under `/projects/:id/qa`, then `authz.Require("qa.…")`;
- re-checks that every id in the URL or form (section, case, scenario, plan, run, item, issue, attachment) belongs to the project in the URL — a repo lookup that returns another project's row is answered 404. The by-id repo functions look rows up by id alone (RLS pins only the organization), so use `repo.QAProjectOf(ctx, s, kind, id)` (or `repo.QAAttachmentProjectID` for files) and compare it with `:id` before calling them;
- is a form POST answered with a redirect + flash, or an htmx fragment when `HX-Request` is set (see `internal/web/folders.go`);
- writes `audit_events` for destructive or sensitive actions (archive, close, delete attachment, share link, sign-off, member changes).

`projectMemberCheck(d)` is a small closure in `internal/web/qa_access.go` wrapping `repo.IsProjectMember` with `tenant.FromContext`.

### Phase 4 — Library and scenarios UI

**Detailed plan:** `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-27-qa-phase-4.md` (validated; where it differs from this outline, its Rulings say why). It also removes the per-metric sidebar entries and makes QA Tools open `/qa`.

| Task | Deliverable | Tests that must exist |
|---|---|---|
| 4.1 QA shell | A "QA" tab on `/projects/:id` and `/projects/:id/qa` landing (counts: cases, scenarios, open plans, open issues). `ProjectDeletionImpactOf` counts QA rows so the delete dialog mentions them. | Route coverage passes; guest on another project gets 404; delete-impact includes case/run/issue counts. |
| 4.2 Section tree + case list | `/projects/:id/qa/cases?section=` — tree on the left (create, rename, move, archive via dialogs like folders), case table (key, title, priority, steps count, updated), search box, archived toggle. Bulk select → move to section. | Create/rename/move/archive happy paths; cycle and depth errors show the repo copy; bulk move ignores ids of other projects; HTML in section and case names renders escaped. |
| 4.3 Case editor | `/projects/:id/qa/cases/new`, `/cases/:key` — title, section picker (`lotus_select`), precondition, notes, priority, negative, steps editor (Alpine list: add, remove, reorder, each with action + expected). Version shown; "Changed since run" hint is Phase 5. | Save creates `TC-n`; edit bumps version only on content change; 500-char title limit message; steps with `<script>` stored verbatim and rendered escaped. |
| 4.4 CSV import/export of cases | Upload CSV (`section path, title, precondition, steps, expected, priority, negative`; steps and expected split on newlines, paired by line) → preview (rows, new sections to create, errors per row) → confirm. Export the current section (or whole project) in the same format. Sections are created by path (`Auth / Login`). | Round trip export → import creates identical cases; row errors listed without importing anything; 5,000-row file completes in one request under 10 s on dev. |
| 4.5 Scenarios | `/projects/:id/qa/scenarios` (list with phase badge and case count) and `/scenarios/:id` — name, phase, description, groups (add, rename, reorder), cases per group (add via a case picker filtered by section/search, remove, drag reorder → `ReorderQAScenario`). Archived cases shown struck through with a warning. "Create scenario from run" (from Phase 5 run page) seeds a scenario from a run's case set. | Add/remove/reorder; duplicate add is a no-op; case of another project rejected; archived case warning; e2e: build a UAT scenario with two groups. |

### Phase 5 — Plans, runs, execution

**Detailed plan:** `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-27-qa-phase-5.md` (validated; where it differs from this outline, its Rulings say why).

| Task | Deliverable | Tests that must exist |
|---|---|---|
| 5.1 Plans | `/projects/:id/qa/plans` (open first; phase, environment, build, window, runs, progress bar) and `/plans/:id` (scenarios, runs by cycle, sign-offs placeholder). Create/edit form with scenario multi-select; "Duplicate plan"; "Close plan". | Duplicate copies scope not dates; closed plan refuses new runs with the repo message; phase filter. |
| 5.2 Create run | From a plan: name, scenario subset, testers multi-select (org members + the project's guests), distribution (none / round-robin / by group) with a live preview of who gets how many. | Each distribution mode; empty scope → "The run would have no cases."; guest cannot create runs (403). |
| 5.3 Execution view | `/projects/:id/qa/runs/:runID` — grouped by `group_name` (collapsible, per-group progress), filters (mine, unassigned, status, group), keyboard-friendly result entry: Pass / Fail / Blocked / Skip + comment + attachments (multipart → `SaveQAAttachment`), result history per item, "Case changed since this run" badge when `qa_cases.version > case_version`. Header shows progress overall and per tester. | Recording each status; untested cannot be submitted; closed run refuses results; attachment over 20 MB refused with message; progress numbers match `QARunProgress`. |
| 5.4 Bulk assignment | Select rows (or a whole group) → assign to a person / unassign, one request → `AssignQARunItems`. | 200 items assigned in one request; ids from another run ignored; guest can't assign. |
| 5.5 Retest and close | "Retest failed & blocked" → `CreateQARetest` (link back to the origin run); "Close run". Run list shows cycle numbers and retest chains. | Retest contains only failed/blocked, current case text, original executor; closing blocks results. |
| 5.6 My work | `/qa/my` — untested items assigned to me across projects (guest: only its projects), grouped by project → plan → run, with a direct "record result" link. Sidebar entry with a count. | Guest sees only its projects; closed runs/plans excluded; count matches `ListMyQAItems`. |

### Phase 6 — Issues

**Detailed plan:** `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-27-qa-phase-6.md` (validated; where it differs from this outline, its Rulings say why).

| Task | Deliverable | Tests that must exist |
|---|---|---|
| 6.1 Issue list | `/projects/:id/qa/issues` — filters (status group open/closed, severity, source, plan, assignee), columns key/title/severity/status/assignee/plan/updated, paged 100 per page like the case library (production has up to 579 issues in one project). | Filters; client-source badge; HTML-escaped titles. |
| 6.2 Report issue | From an item (pre-filled title "Fail: <case title>", steps from the snapshot, environment and plan from the run's plan, link created) or standalone. Guests create with `source=client` (`qa.issue.report`). | From-item link and plan; guest issue is `client`; member issue is `internal`; severity required. |
| 6.3 Issue page | `/projects/:id/qa/issues/:key` — fields (editable with `qa.issue.write`), status change with note, timeline of comments and status events, attachments on issue and comments, linked items (with run + status). | Status change writes one event; unchanged status writes none; guest can comment but not change status/severity; attachment download checks project access. |
| 6.4 Attachment download | `GET /projects/:id/qa/files/:attachmentID` — `QAAttachmentProjectID` must equal `:id`, streams from the blob store with `Content-Disposition: attachment` for non-images and `inline` for images; `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`. | Cross-project id → 404; guest of another project → 404; HTML file served as attachment, never inline. |

### Phase 7 — Guest UAT

**Detailed plan:** `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-27-qa-phase-7.md` (validated; where it differs from this outline, its Rulings say why).

| Task | Deliverable | Tests that must exist |
|---|---|---|
| 7.1 Guest role in settings | Members screen offers `guest`; invitation form gets a project picker when role is guest (writes `project_members` on accept). "Project members" panel on the project page (`project_member.manage`) to add/remove guests. | Invite guest to 2 projects → sees exactly those; removing a project hides it immediately; admin can change guest ↔ member. |
| 7.2 Guest shell | A guest's home is `/qa/my`; sidebar shows only its projects' QA tabs; every non-QA page (dashboard, metrics, settings except own tokens) answers 404 for guests. | Crawl every registered GET route as a guest (reuse the route-coverage table): only QA routes of its projects answer 200. |
| 7.3 Sign-off | On a plan: guest or admin records `accepted` / `accepted_with_notes` / `rejected` per scenario or for the whole plan, with a note; shown on the plan page and in the report; audit event. | Guest can sign off only its projects' plans; member without `qa.signoff` gets 403; latest sign-off per scenario shown. |
| 7.4 Share links and report | `qa.share` creates a read-only plan report link (`/s/qa/:token`, `authz.Public` with its own token check): scope, runs, progress per scenario, open issues, sign-offs. Token stored hashed, 30-day default expiry, revocable. Also "Export report" (PDF-friendly print CSS). | Expired/revoked token → 404; the report exposes no write form and no attachment URLs outside the plan; token cannot be used on any other route. |

### Phase 8 — API/MCP, integrations, cut-over

**Detailed plan (8.1, 8.2):** `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-qa-phase-8-api-mcp.md` (validated; where it differs from this outline, its Decisions say why).

| Task | Deliverable | Tests that must exist |
|---|---|---|
| 8.1 JSON API | `/api/qa/...` authenticated by Lotus API tokens (`KindToken`, scopes = qa permissions): list projects, sections, cases (paged), create cases in bulk (≤ 500 per request, section by path), scenarios, plans, runs, items, record result, create issue. | Token scope narrower than role is enforced; bulk create is atomic; keys sequential under concurrent bulk calls. |
| 8.2 MCP endpoint | `POST /mcp/qa` speaking MCP streamable HTTP over the 8.1 operations (tools: `list_projects`, `list_sections`, `list_cases`, `create_cases`, `list_plans`, `get_run`, `record_result`, `create_issue`). Legacy QA Tools had 33 MCP tokens and bulk AI case creation drove 73% of new cases — this keeps that workflow. | Tool list; each tool maps to the API with the same permission checks; unauthenticated call → 401 (the legacy `X-Public` flaw must not reappear). |
| 8.3 ceo-monitor summary | `GET /api/qa/summary?project=<slug|id>` (service token): the figures ceo-monitor's `projects/services/qa_tools.py` computes today (per-run pass-rate average, totals, distinct cases clean/failed/untested, run count, last run, optional period). Hand ceo-monitor a change to call this instead of the legacy DB. | Numbers equal ceo-monitor's current SQL on the rehearsal data for 5 sample projects. |
| ~~8.4 Legacy URL redirects~~ | **Dropped (user decision, 2026-09-28).** Old `qa.javan.id` links are not redirected; they stop working when QA Tools is decommissioned. | — |
| 8.5 Cut-over | Execute `docs/qa-import-runbook.md` "Cut-over"; after 90 days, a migration dropping `qa_legacy_ids`. | Runbook checklist ticked in the collab task. |

## Self-review

1. **Spec coverage** — §3 hierarchy: Tasks 3–9. §4 tables: Task 3 (all 17 + `project_members` in Task 1). §5 permissions: Tasks 1–2, enforced per route in Phases 4–7. §6 dropped fields: reflected in the schema (Task 3) and the mapping (Task 13). §7 migration rules: Tasks 12–19, each rule pinned by a fixture row. §8 rollout: Task 19 + Phase 8. Out of scope items (§10) have no task, deliberately.
2. **Placeholders** — Phases 1–3 contain complete code and tests. Phases 4–8 are explicitly outlines to be expanded with superpowers:writing-plans before execution; they name deliverables and required tests, not code.
3. **Type consistency** — `QASteps`, `QACase`, `QARunItem`, `QAResult`, `QAIssue`, `QAIssueEvent`, `QAAttachment`, `QAPlan`, `QARun` are defined once (Tasks 4–11) and reused verbatim by the import writer (Task 14) and the importer (Tasks 15–17). Repo errors: `ErrNotFound`, `ErrInvalidInput` (existing), `ErrQANameTaken`, `ErrQASectionCycle`, `ErrQASectionTooDeep`, `ErrQAClosed`, `ErrQAEmptyRun`, `ErrQAAttachmentTooLarge`.
4. **Review Focus** — (1) guest cross-project: Task 2 test + Phase 5–7 route tests; (2) concurrent keys: Task 5 test; (3) snapshot vs retest text: Task 7 tests; (4) concurrent imports: Task 16 test, kill-and-resume: Task 19; (5) HTML/non-ASCII: fixture rows c32/b3 in Task 12, asserted in Task 15; rendering in Phase 4.
5. **Validation** — corrections found by executing Phases 1–3: `control_plane_test.go` role count (Task 1); `authz.SetPrincipal` already exists and `AddProjectMember` must check project visibility before `ON CONFLICT` (Task 2); four foreign keys made deferrable so project and organization deletes cascade (Task 3); `COALESCE` on depth queries, parent sections and scenario groups checked against their project/scenario (Tasks 4, 6); fixture helper moved to `legacyqatest` (Task 12); `LockLegacy` against concurrent imports, `EnsureProject` never merges unmapped projects, renumbered rows keep `Legacy code: …`, dry run writes no blobs and reports real user/membership counts, `verify` checks organization-wide project totals (Tasks 14–18); Task 19 numbers replaced with measured ones.
